    Notes:                                                                                     附註：
    (1)    Net profit margin is profit for the year divided by revenue.                        (1) 淨利潤率指年內溢利除收入。
    (2)    Return on total assets is profit for the year divided by average total assets.      (2) 總資產回報率指年內溢利除平均資產總額。
    (3)    Return on capital employed is the ratio of profit before income tax expense         (3) 淨資本回報率指利息及稅項前盈利除總資產減流動負債。
           and interest divided by total assets less current liabilities.
    (4)    Liabilities-to-Adjusted EBITDA ratio is the ratio of total liabilities divided by   (4)    負債對經調整 EBITDA 倍數指總負債除經調整利息、稅
           adjusted EBITDA.                                                                           項、折舊及攤銷前盈利。
    (5)    Debts-to-net assets ratio is the ratio of the sum of interest-bearing               (5)    淨資產負債率指計息借貸（包括可轉換債券（如有））與租
           borrowings (including convertible bonds, if any) and lease liabilities divided             賃負債之和除以總權益。
           by total equity.
    (6)    Gearing ratio is the ratio of the sum of interest-bearing borrowings,               (6)    資本負債率指計息借貸（包括可轉換債券（如有））與租
           (including convertible bonds, if any) and lease liabilities divided by the sum             賃負債之和除以總權益、計息借貸（包括可轉換債券（如
           of total equity, interest-bearing borrowings (including convertible bonds, if              有））與租賃負債之和。
           any) and lease liability.
    (7)    Debts-to-assets ratio is the ratio of the sum of interest-bearing borrowings        (7)    資產負債率指計息借貸（包括可轉換債券（如有））與租賃
           (including convertible bonds, if any) and lease liabilities divided by total               負債之和除以總資產。
           assets.
    (8)    Average finance cost is the ratio of aggregated amount of each category of          (8)    平均融資成本指各種類借貸成本乘其佔總借貸比例，然
           borrowing multiply by its interest rate divided by the total borrowing.                    後加總。
    (9)    Due to business combinations under common control completed in 2017,                (9)    由於二零一七年完成共同控制下的業務合併，本集團的
           the financial information of the Group has been restated to include the                    財務資料已重列，按合併基準包括中石油京唐液化天然
           financial position and results of operation of PetroChina Jingtang LNG Co.,                氣有限公司（「京唐公司」）的財務狀況及業績。
           Ltd. (“Jingtang Co.”) on a combined basis.
    (10)   The financial information of the Group has not been restated for the                (10)   本集團的財務資料並未就收購京唐公司而重列，原因是
           acquisition of Jingtang Co. as the directors consider that the unrestated                  董事認為，未重列的財務資料更適合對本集團業務經營
           financial information is more appropriate for year-on-year comparison of                   的變動按年進行比較。
           the change in the Group’s business operation.
    (11)   The financial information of the Group has not been restated for the                (11)   本集團的財務資料並未就收購京唐公司及中石油昆侖燃
           acquisition of Jingtang Co. and PetroChina Kunlun Gas Co., Ltd. (“Kunlun                   氣有限公司（「昆侖燃氣」）而重列，原因是董事認為，未
           Gas”) as the directors consider that the unrestated financial information                  重列的財務資料更適合對本集團業務經營的變動按年進
           is more appropriate for year-on-year comparison of the change in the                       行比較。
           Group’s business operation.
    (12)   Core profit before income tax expense is defined as profit before                   (12)   除所得稅費用前核心溢利指不包括物業、廠房及設備、
           income tax expense excluding impairment losses on property, plant                          無形資產、應收賬款及其他應收款項減值虧損及匯兌虧
           and equipment, intangible assets, accounts and other receivables and                       損之除所得稅費用前溢利。
           exchange loss.
    (13)   Core profit attributable to owners of the Company is defined as profit              (13)   本公司股東應佔核心溢利指不包括本公司股東應佔之物
           attributable to owners of the Company excluding impairment losses on                       業、廠房及設備、無形資產、應收賬款及其他應收款項
           property, plant and equipment, intangible assets, accounts and other                       減值虧損及匯兌虧損及其有關稅務影響之本公司股東應
           receivables and exchange loss attributable to owners of the Company and                    佔溢利。
           its related tax effect.
    (14)   Adjusted EBITDA is defined as profit before income tax expense, excluding           (14)   經調整扣除利息、稅項、折舊及攤銷前盈利指不包括物
           impairment loss on property, plant and equipment, impairment loss on                       業、廠房及設備減值虧損、無形資產減值虧損、應收賬
           intangible assets, impairment loss on accounts and other receivables,                      款及其他應收款項減值虧損、匯兌虧損、利息以及折
           exchange loss, interest and depreciation, depletion and amortisation.                      舊、損耗及攤銷之除所得稅費用前溢利。
    (15)   The Group has initially applied HKFRS 16 at 1 January 2019 using the                (15)   於二零一九年一月一日，本集團已使用經修訂追溯法初
           modified retrospective approach. Under this approach, comparative                          步應用香港財務報告準則第 16 號。根據此方法，可比較
           information is not restated. Please see Note 3(z) in the Notes to the                      資料並無重列，請參閱綜合財務報表附註之附註 3(z)。

The revenue of the Group reached RMB113,313 million,
representing a year-on-year increase of RMB7,843 million or
7.44%; the profit attributable to owners of the Company was
RMB5,551 million, representing a year-on-year increase of
RMB917 million or 19.79%. Earnings per share (basic) was
RMB66.71 cents.

本集團實現銷售收入達人民幣 1,133.13 億元，同比增加人民幣
78.43 億元或 7.44%；本公司股東應佔溢利人民幣 55.51 億元，
同比增加人民幣 9.17 億元或 19.79%。每股盈利（基本）為人民幣
66.71 分。

                                                                                        二零一九年年度報告
     Management Discussion
       and Analysis
                管理層討論與分析

Kunlun Energy Company Limited and its subsidiaries continued to
develop its natural gas business segment during the year ended
31 December 2019. Profit before income tax expense of the Group
for the Year was approximately RMB13,645 million, representing
an increase of 13.85% as compared with RMB11,985 million for
the last year. Profit attributable to owners of the Company for
the Year was approximately RMB5,551 million, representing an
increase of 19.79% as compared with RMB4,634 million for the
last year.

昆侖能源有限公司及其附屬公司於截至二零一九年十二月三十一
日止年度繼續擴大天然氣業務板塊。本集團於本年度之除所得稅
費用前溢利約為人民幣 13,645 百萬元，比去年金額人民幣 11,985
百萬元增加 13.85%。於本年度本公司股東應佔溢利約為人民幣
5,551 百萬元，比去年金額人民幣 4,634 百萬元增加 19.79%。

Practices and Conduct of Meetings                                      會議常規及操守

Notices of regular Board meetings are served to all Directors          董事會定期會議通知至少於會議前 14 日向全體董事
at least 14 days before the meetings and reasonable notice             發出，而其他董事會會議一般會發出合理通知。委
is generally given for other Board meetings. For committee             員會會議將根據有關職權範圍所訂之所須通知期發
meetings, notices are served in accordance with the required           出通知。
notice period stated in the relevant terms of reference.

Agenda and Board papers together with all appropriate, complete        議程及會議文件連同所有適當、完備及可靠資料至
and reliable information are normally sent to all Directors/           少於各董事會會議或委員會會議舉行前三天送交全
committee members at least 3 days before each Board meeting            體董事╱委員會成員，以令董事知悉本公司之最近
or committee meeting to keep the Directors abreast of the latest       發展及財政狀況，並能夠在掌握有關資料之情況下
developments and financial position of the Company and to              作出決定。需要時，董事會及每名董事亦有個別及
enable them to make informed decisions. The Board and each             獨立途徑接觸高級管理人員。
Director also have separate and independent access to the senior
management whenever necessary.

The Chairman, Chief Executive Officer, President, Chief Financial      需要時，主席、行政總裁、總裁、財務總監及公司
Officer and Company Secretary will attend Board and committee          秘書將出席董事會及委員會會議，以就本公司之業
meetings, when necessary, to advise on business developments,          務發展、財務及會計事宜、法規遵守、企業管治及
financial and accounting matters, statutory compliance, corporate      其他重要方面提供意見。
governance and other major aspects of the Company.

Minutes of all Board meetings and committee meetings are kept          所有董事會會議及委員會會議之會議記錄由公司秘
by the Company Secretary. Minutes are prepared after each              書備存。會議紀錄於各會議後編製，而最後定稿則
meeting and the final version is signed by the Chairman or the         由主席或相關委員會主席（視情況而定）簽署，並於
chairman of the relevant committees (as the case may be) and           下一次董事會會議由董事會或於一下次委員會會議
confirmed by the Board in the following Board Meeting or by the        由相關委員會（視情況而定）確認。經確認之會議紀
relevant committee in the following committee meeting (as the          錄乃備存供日後參照及董事查閱之用。
case may be). The confirmed minutes are kept for future reference
and Directors’ inspection.

According to current Board practice, any material transaction,         根據現行董事會常規，任何涉及主要股東或董事之
which involves a conflict of interests for a substantial shareholder   利益衝突之重大交易，將由董事會於正式召開之董
or a Director, will be considered and dealt with by the Board at       事會上作出考慮及處理。本公司細則亦載有條文，
a duly convened Board meeting. The Company’s Bye-laws also             規定董事於批准該等董事或彼等任何聯繫人士有重
contain provisions requiring Directors to abstain from voting          大利益之交易之會議上放棄投票，且不計入會議法
and not to be counted in the quorum at meetings for approving          定人數。
transactions in which such Directors or any of their associates
have a material interest.

     CHAIRMAN AND CHIEF EXECUTIVE OFFICER                                  主席及行政總裁

     Chief Executive Officer should be separate and should not be          分，並不應由一人同時兼任。主席與行政總裁之間
     performed by the same individual. The division of responsibilities    職責之分工應清楚界定並以書面列載。
     between the Chairman and Chief Executive Officer should be
     clearly established and set out in writing.

     the Chairman of the Board and the Chief Executive Officer to          工，以確保權力和授權分佈均衡。
     ensure a balance of power and authority.

     no relationship between the Chairman and Chief Executive Officer.

     There are written terms on the general division of responsibilities   主席及行政總裁有書面列載一般職責分工範圍。根
     between the Chairman and the Chief Executive Officer. The             據良好企業管治常規，主席作出領導並負責董事會
     Chairman provides leadership and is responsible for the effective     之有效運作。在高級管理人員之支持下，主席同時
     functioning of the Board in accordance with good corporate            負責確保董事適時取得充分、完備及可靠之資料及
     governance practice. With the support of the senior management,       適當知悉董事會會議上之事項。行政總裁集中執行
     the Chairman is also responsible for ensuring that the Directors      董事會批准及轉授之目標、政策及策略。彼負責本
     receive adequate, complete and reliable information in a timely       公司之日常管理及經營。行政總裁同時負責開發策
     manner and appropriate briefing on issues arising at Board            略計劃及制定組織架構、監控系統及內部程序及過
     meetings. The Chief Executive Officer focuses on implementing         程，以供董事會批准。
     objectives, policies and strategies approved and delegated by the

RISK MANAGEMENT AND INTERNAL CONTROLS                                     風險管理及內部監控

It is the responsibility of the Board to ensure that the Company          董事會負責確保本公司之內部監控系統穩健妥善而
maintains sound and effective internal controls to safeguard the          且有效，以保障股東之投資及本公司之資產。本公
shareholders investment and the Company’s assets. The internal            司之內部監控系統包含完善之公司架構以及全面之
control system of the Company comprises a well-established                政策及標準。本公司亦設有內部審計部門，負責監
organisational structure and comprehensive policies and                   控本集團內部管治及監督本集團風險管理及內部控
standards. The Company also has an internal audit department,             制系統。
which is responsible for monitoring the internal governance of the
Group and overseeing the risk management and internal control
system of the Group.

The Board, through the Audit Committee, assesses annually                 董事會透過審核委員會每年評核本集團內部監控系
the effectiveness of the Group’s internal control system which            統之效能。該系統涵蓋所有重要監控，包括本公司
covers all material controls, including financial, operational and        的財務、營運及遵守法規的監控以及風險管理。董

     Opinion                                                            意見

     We have audited the consolidated financial statements of Kunlun    本核數師（以下簡稱「我們」）已審核列載於第 89 頁
     Energy Company Limited (the “Company”) and its subsidiaries        至第 245 頁昆侖能源有限公司（以下簡稱「貴公司」）
     (the “Group”) set out on pages 89 to 245, which comprise the       及其附屬公司（統稱「貴集團」）的綜合財務報表，此
     consolidated statement of financial position as at 31 December     綜合財務報表包括於二零一九年十二月三十一日的
     2019, the consolidated statement of comprehensive income, the      綜合財務狀況表與截至該日止年度的綜合全面收益
     consolidated statement of changes in equity and the consolidated   表、綜合權益變動表和綜合現金流量表，以及綜合
     statement of cash flows for the year then ended and notes to       財務報表附註，包括主要會計政策概要。
     the consolidated financial statements, including a summary of
     significant accounting policies.

     In our opinion, the consolidated financial statements give a       我們認為，該等綜合財務報表已根據香港會計師
     true and fair view of the consolidated financial position of the   公會（「香港會計師公會」）頒佈的《香港財務報告準
     Group as at 31 December 2019 and of its consolidated financial     則》（「香港財務報告準則」）真實而中肯地反映 貴
     performance and its consolidated cash flows for the year then      集團於二零一九年十二月三十一日的綜合財務狀況
     ended in accordance with Hong Kong Financial Reporting             及 貴集團截至該日止年度的綜合財務表現及綜合
     Standards (“HKFRSs”) issued by the Hong Kong Institute of          現金流量，並已遵照香港《公司條例》的披露要求妥
     Certified Public Accountants (“HKICPA”) and have been properly     為擬備。
     prepared in compliance with the disclosure requirements of the
     Hong Kong Companies Ordinance.

     circumstances, we determine that a matter should not be                 定不應在報告中溝通該事項。
     communicated in our report because the adverse consequences
     of doing so would reasonably be expected to outweigh the public
     interest benefits of such communication.

          An associate is an entity in which the Group or                    聯營公司指本集團或本公司對其有重
          Company has significant influence, but not control                 大影響力，但無法控制或共同控制其
          or joint control, over its management, including                   管理（包括參與財務及經營政策決定）
          participation in the financial and operating policy                之實體。
          decisions.

          A joint venture is an arrangement whereby the                      合資企業為一項安排，據此本集團或
          Group or Company and other parties contractually                   公司及其他訂約方同意共同控制該安
          agree to share control of the arrangement, and have                排，及對該安排之淨資產享有權利。
          rights to the net assets of the arrangement.

                 based upon future conditions. The cost shall be that
                 prevailing at the end of the period.

                       Measurement of ECLs (Continued)                                      計量預期信貸虧損（續）

                       Loss allowances for accounts receivable,                             應收賬款、租賃應收款項及合
                       lease receivables and contract assets,                               約資產的虧損撥備一般是以整

                       experience, adjusted for factors that are                            行估算，並在報告當日按債務
                       specific to the debtors and an assessment                            人的個別因素及對當前和預測
                       of both the current and forecast general                             整體經濟狀況的評估進行調
                       economic conditions at the reporting date.                           整。

                       For all other financial instruments, the                             至於所有其他金融工具，本集
                       Group recognises a loss allowance equal                              團會以相等於 12 個月的預期信
                       to 12-month ECLs unless there has been                               貸虧損金額確認虧損撥備，除
                       a significant increase in credit risk of the                         非自初始確認後該金融工具的

                       at an amount equal to lifetime ECLs.                                 的預期信貸虧損金額計量。

          convertible bonds is measured at fair value based                  分按公平值根據未來利息及本金還款

          discounted at the prevailing market rate of interest               市場利率折現。權益部分為整體可轉
          for similar non-convertible instruments. The equity                換債券之初始公平值與負債部分之初
          component is the difference between the initial                    始公平值之間之差額。有關發行複合
          fair value of the convertible bonds as a whole                     金融工具的交易成本按所得款項的比
          and the initial fair value of the liability component.             例分配至負債及權益部分。
          Transaction costs that relate to the issue of a
          compound financial instrument are allocated to the
          liability and equity components in proportion to the
          allocation of proceeds.

          The liability component is subsequently carried at                 負債部分其後按攤銷成本列賬。就負
          amortised cost. Interest expense recognised in profit              債部分於損益內確認的利息開支乃使
          or loss on the liability component is calculated using             用實際利率法計算。權益部分會於可
          the effective interest method. The equity component                轉換債券權益儲備確認，直至債券被
          is recognised in the convertible bond equity reserve               轉換或被贖回為止。
          until either the bonds are converted or redeemed.

          If the bonds are converted, the convertible bond                   倘債券獲轉換，可轉換債券權益儲備
          equity reserve, together with the carrying amount of               及負債部分賬面值於轉換時轉撥至股
          the liability component at the time of conversion, is              本及股份溢價作為已發行股份的代
          transferred to share capital and share premium as                  價。倘債券被贖回，則可轉換債券權
          consideration for the shares issued. If the bonds are              益儲備將直接轉入滾存盈利。
          redeemed, the convertible bond equity reserve is
          released directly to retained earnings.

    (o)   Cash and cash equivalents                                    (o)   現金及現金等值項目

          Cash and cash equivalents comprise cash at bank                    現金及現金等值項目包括銀行及手頭
          and on hand, deposits held with banks and highly                   現金、存放於銀行之存款以及可隨時
          liquid investments that are readily convertible into               兌換成可知數額之現金，並承受極低
          known amounts of cash and which are subject                        價值變動風險及於購入時三個月內到
          to an insignificant risk of changes in value, having               期之高流通性投資。現金及現金等值
          been within three months of maturity at acquisition.               項目根據附註 3(m)(i) 所載之政策評估

                 are recorded as deferred income, and recognised                     確認為遞延收益，並在相關資產可使

                 are recorded as deferred income, and recognised                     償本集團以後期間的相關費用或損失
                 as income in future periods as compensation when                    的，本集團將其確認為遞延收益，並
                 the associated future expenses or losses arise; or                  在確認相關費用的期間計入損益；如
                 directly recognised as income in the current period                 果用於補償本集團已發生的相關費用
                 as compensation for past expenses or losses.                        或損失的，則直接計入當期收益。

          (ii) it is probable that an outflow of resources will                成本及法律索償撥備。

          the likelihood that an outflow will be required in                   導致資源流出的可能性乃透過整體考

          money and the risks specific to the obligation. The                  產生之撥備增加乃確認為利息支出。
          increase in the provision due to passage of time is
          recognised as interest expense.

                 a.    New definition of a lease                                    a.   租賃之新定義

                       The change in the definition of a lease mainly                    租賃定義之變動主要與控制權
                       relates to the concept of control. HKFRS                          概念有關。香港財務報告準則
                       16 defines a lease on the basis of whether                        第 16 號根據客戶是否於一段時
                       a customer controls the use of an identified                      間內控制已識別資產之使用來
                       asset for a period of time, which may be                          定義租賃，而這可藉指定之使
                       determined by a defined amount of use.                            用量釐定。倘客戶既有權指示
                       Control is conveyed where the customer                            已識別資產之使用又有權獲取
                       has both the right to direct the use of the                       有關使用帶來之絕大部分經濟
                       identified asset and to obtain substantially all                  利益，則控制權已被轉移。
                       of the economic benefits from that use.

                       The Group applies the new definition of a                         本集團僅對在二零一九年一月
                       lease in HKFRS 16 only to contracts that                          一日或之後訂立或變更之合約

                       1 January 2019. For contracts entered                             之租賃新定義。對在二零一九
                       into before 1 January 2019, the Group has                         年一月一日之前訂立之合約，
                       used the transitional practical expedient                         本集團已使用過渡性實際權宜
                       to grandfather the previous assessment of                         方法保留現有安排是否屬於
                       which existing arrangements are or contain                        （或包含）租賃之先前評估。因
                       leases. Accordingly, contracts that were                          此，先前根據香港會計準則第
                       previously assessed as leases under HKAS                          17 號評估為租賃之合約繼續根
                       17 continue to be accounted for as leases                         據香港財務報告準則第 16 號以
                       under HKFRS 16 and contracts previously                           租賃入賬，而先前評估為非租
                       assessed as non-lease service arrangements                        賃服務安排之合約則繼續以待

                all outstanding balances before any further

                different loss patterns for different customer                    現顯著不同的虧損模式，故就

                past due status is distinguished between                          分根據逾期情況作出之虧損撥

                the customer bases are separated into                             方及第三方。通過考慮關連方
                the related parties and third parties. The                        的財務狀況，過往經驗及其他

                 short-term and long-term borrowings.

                 bonds, if any) and lease liabilities divided by the sum             可轉換債券（如有））及租賃負債之和
                 of total equity, interest-bearing borrowings (including             計算監察資本。於二零一九年十二月
                 convertible bonds, if any) and lease liabilities. The               三十一日之資本負債率為 30.72%（二

    Estimates and judgements are regularly evaluated by the            本集團定期根據過往經驗及其他因素（包括
    Group, based on historical experience and other factors            對未來事件在當前情況下認為合理之預期）
    which include expectations of future events that are               對估計及判斷進行評估。
    believed to be reasonable under the circumstances.

    Significant sources of estimation uncertainty and                  估計不明朗因素及判斷之重大來源載述下
    judgement are described as follows:                                文：
    (a)   Estimation of oil and natural gas reserves                   (a)   石油及天然氣儲量之估計
          Estimates of oil and natural gas reserves are key                  石油及天然氣儲量之估計對本集團之
          elements in the Group’s investment decision-making                 投資決策程序至關重要，同時亦是進
          process. They are also an important element in                     行減值測試之重要因素。探明石油及
          testing for impairment. Changes in proved oil and                  天然氣儲量之變化，尤其是探明已開
          natural gas reserves, particularly proved developed                發儲量，將影響於本集團綜合財務報
          reserves, will affect unit-of-production depreciation,             表就與石油及天然氣生產活動相關之
          depletion and amortisation recorded in the Group’s                 物業、廠房及設備所記錄之單位產量
          consolidated financial statements for property, plant              折舊、損耗及攤銷。探明已開發儲量
          and equipment related to oil and gas production                    之 減 少 將 增 加 折 舊、 損 耗 及 攤 銷 金
          activities. A reduction in proved developed reserves               額。探明儲量估計可根據新資料作出
          will increase depreciation, depletion and amortisation             向上或向下修訂，例如，來自開發鑽
          charges. Proved reserve estimates are subject to                   探及生產活動或來自經濟因素之變化

          information, such as from development drilling and                 或開發計劃等。
          production activities or from changes in economic

                 activities in the consolidated statement of cash                    「經營活動」。根據香港財務報告準

                 lease payments, payments for leases of low value                     不會計入之短期租賃付款、低價值
                 assets and variable lease payments not included                      資產租賃付款及可變租賃付款外，
                 in the measurement of lease liabilities, all other                   所有其他已付租金現時均分拆為本
                 rentals paid on leases are now split into capital                    金部分及利息部分（見附註 26(b)）
                 element and interest element (see Note 26(b)) and                    並分類為融資現金流出。根據經修
                 classified as financing cash outflows. Under the                     訂追溯法，比較資料並無重列。有
                 modified retrospective approach, the comparative                     關過渡至香港財務報告準則第 16

           The average credit period on purchase of goods is 90                   採購貨品之平均賒賬期為 90 日。本集團已制
           days. The Group has financial risk management policies in              定財務風險管理政策，以確保所有應付款項

35   OIL PRODUCTION SHARING CONTRACT                                35   油田產品分成合同

     Leng Jiapu Contract                                                 冷家堡合同

     Pursuant to the Leng Jiapu Contract signed in 1997, the             根據簽訂於一九九七年的冷家堡合同，本集
     Group agreed to acquire 70% of the production sharing               團同意收購 70% 之產品分成權益，作價人民
     interest for RMB1,008 million and to fund its share of              幣 1,008 百萬元，並負擔於冷家堡合同所界
     cost of the development carried out for the realisation             定之地區（「冷家堡合同地區」）內就生產石油
     of oil production (the “Development Operations”) in the             產品（「開發作業」）應佔之開發成本，首兩年
     area as defined in the Leng Jiapu Contract (the “LJP                開發期之估計成本為 65.5 百萬美元（約人民
     Contract Area”), at an estimated cost of US$65.5 million            幣 474 百萬元），此外再負責首兩年後 70%
     (approximately RMB474 million) in the first two years of the        之開發成本，以取得冷家堡合同地區 70% 之
     development period and be further responsible for 70% of            產油量。
     the development cost after the first two years, in exchange
     for a 70% share in the oil production from the LJP Contract
     Area.

     Pursuant to the Leng Jiapu Contract, the Group shall bear           根據冷家堡合同，本集團負擔冷家堡合同地
     70% of the costs required for the Development Operations            區內開發作業及生產分成所需之 70% 成本，
     in the LJP Contract Area which shall be allocated (after            產量分配為（經扣除當地稅項及企業所得稅
     deduction of local taxes and enterprise income tax) firstly         後）先用於收回經營成本，其餘由本集團與
     towards operating costs recovery and thereafter in the              中國石油按 70% 與 30% 之比例攤分，作為
     proportion of 70% to the Group and 30% to PetroChina                收回投資成本及賺取的利潤。
     towards investment recovery and profit.

     The Leng Jiapu Contract provides twenty consecutive                 冷家堡合同規定，由開始商業作業日期起計
     years of production sharing from the date of                        連續分佔石油產量二十年並將於二零二三年
     commencement of commercial production and will expire               十二月三十一日屆滿。
     on 31 December 2023.

     has also entered into an Entrustment Contract with an               團全資擁有並營運之業務實體訂立委託合
     operational entity owned and operated by CNPC, whereby              同，據此，後者獲委託承擔作為作業者的責
     the latter is entrusted to take up the responsibility as            任。根據委託合同，已成立聯合開發管理組

     ventures, as all of which are managed on a central basis.          上各項均集中管理。

      39.   NON-ADJUSTING EVENT AFTER THE                                   39   報告期後非調整事項
            REPORTING PERIOD

            The outbreak of novel coronavirus disease (COVID-19)                 於二零二零年初爆發新型冠狀病毒
            epidemic in early 2020 has led to a series of precautionary          (COVID-19) 疫 情 已 導 致 全 球 各 地 實 施 一 系
            and control measures implemented across the globe                    列預防及控制措施，亦已對國內外能源價格
            and also casted downward pressure on domestic and                    造成下調壓力。此可能會對本集團之業務營
            international energy prices. It may have certain impacts             運產生若干影響。本集團將密切留意疫情之
            on the Group’s business operations. The Group will pay               發展以及評估其對財務狀況、現金流量及經
            close attention to the development of the epidemic and               營業績之影響。截至該等財務報表刊發之日
            assess its impact on the financial position, cash flows and          期，評估仍在進行中，且相關影響在現階段
            operating results. Up to the date of issue of these financial        未能作合理估計。
            statements, the assessment is still in progress and the
            related impact could not be reasonably estimated at this
            stage.

      Reserve Quantities Information
      儲量資料


      INFORMATION ON EXPLORATION AND                                        有關勘探與生產板塊之資料
      PRODUCTION SEGMENT

      In accordance with the Listing Rule 18.18, this section provides      根據上市規則第 18.18 條，本節提供有關本集團油
      supplemental information on oil and gas producing activities of the   氣生產活動的補充資料。
      Group.

Definition:                                                            釋義：

The petroleum reserves are classified as follows:                      石油儲量分類如下：

Proved oil and gas reserves – Proved oil and gas reserves are          探明油氣儲量 － 探明油氣儲量為自給定日期至合
those quantities of oil and gas, which by analysis of geoscience       同約定權利到期日（除非有證據合理保證該權利能
and engineering data, can be estimated with reasonable certainty       夠得到延期），透過地球科學和工程數據的分析，
to be economically producible – from a given date forward, from        採用確定性或概率性評估，以現有經濟、作業和政
known reservoirs, and under existing economic conditions,              府管制條件，可以合理確定已知油氣藏經濟可採油
operating methods, and government regulations – prior to the           氣的數量。項目須已開始萃取烴或作業者須合理確
time at which contracts providing the right to operate expire,         定其將於合理時間內開始項目。
unless evidence indicates that renewal is reasonably certain,
regardless of whether deterministic or probabilistic methods are
used for the estimation. The project to extract the hydrocarbons
must have commenced or the operator must be reasonably
certain that it will commence the project within a reasonable time.

      (i)     The areas of the reservoir considered as proved includes:         (i)     可視為已探明油氣藏的區域包括：

              (A) The area identified by drilling and limited by fluid                  (A) 鑽井劃定及流體介面圈定（如有）的地區，
              contracts, if any, and (B) Adjacent undrilled portions of the             及 (B) 根據可用地質及工程資料可合理斷定
              reservoir that can, with reasonable certainty, be judged                  其可以持續用於一定經濟可開採油氣的油氣
              to be continuous with it and to contain economically                      藏附近未鑽探部份。
              producible oil or gas on the basis of available geoscience
              and engineering data.

      (ii)    In the absence of data on fluid contracts, proved quantities      (ii)    若缺乏流體介面資料，除非地質、工程或表
              in a reservoir are limited by the lowest known hydrocarbons               現資料及可靠技術確定合理存在較低含量，
              (LKH) as seen in a well penetration unless geoscience,                    否則油氣藏內之探明儲量乃根據油井滲透所
              engineering, or performance data and reliable technology                  示的最低探明含烴量 (LKH) 而定。
              establishes a lower contact with reasonable certainty.

      (iii)   Where direct observation from well penetrations has               (iii)   當透過井眼直接觀測已確定最高探明含油量
              defined a highest known oil (HKO) elevation and the                       高位 (HKO) 及存在潛在相關天然氣上限，則
              potential exists for an associated gas cap, proved oil                    除非地質、工程或表現資料及可靠技術確定
              reserves may be assigned in the structurally higher                       合理存在較高含量，否則探明石油儲量可分
              portions of the reservoir only if geoscience, engineering,                派至油氣藏構造較高的地區。
              or performance data and reliable technology establish the
              higher contact with reasonable certainty.

      (iv)    Reserves which can be produced economically through               (iv)    通過應用提高採收率技術（包括但不限於注
              application of improved recovery techniques (including,                   流體）可以經濟採出的儲量包括在探明儲量
              but not limited to, fluid injection) are included in the proved           之列，倘：
              classification when:

              (A) Successful testing by a pilot project in an area of the               (A) 一旦油氣藏（其物業不如整體油氣藏優越）
              reservoir with properties no more favorable than in the                   的先導性試驗項目試驗成功、油氣藏或類似
              reservoir as a whole, the operation of an installed program               油氣藏既定方案作業成功或使用可靠技術的
              in the reservoir or an analogous reservoir, or other evidence             其他證據探明項目和方案所依據的工程方法
              using reliable technology establishes the reasonable                      合理可靠的話；及 (B) 該項目已獲所有必要人
              certainty of the engineering analysis on which the project                士及實體（包括政府實體）批准進行開發。
              or program was based; and (B) The project has been
              approved for development by all necessary parties and
              entities, including governmental entities.

      (v)     Existing economic conditions include prices and costs             (v)     現有的經濟條件包含確定一個油氣藏經濟生
              at which economic producibility from a reservoir is to be                 產能力的價格和成本。除非由合同約定，該
              determined. The price shall be the average price during                   價格是指在本報告期截止日以前的十二個月
              the 12-month period prior to the ending date of the period                的算術平均價格，乃確定為該期間內每個月
              covered by the report, determined as an unweighted                        第一天價格的未加權算術平均數，但不包括
              arithmetic average of the first-day-of-the-month price for                基於未來條件做出的價格調整。
              each month within such period, unless prices are defined

Developed oil and gas reserves – Developed oil and gas reserves         已開發油氣儲量 － 已開發油氣儲量為預期可透過
are reserves of any category that can be expected to be                 以下方式開採的儲量：
recovered:

(i)     Through existing wells with existing equipment and              (i)     利用現有設備和作業方法，或者開採儲量所
        operating methods or in which the cost of the required                  需的開發設備成本明顯低於鑽探一口新井所
        equipment is relatively minor compared to the cost of a                 需成本，可從現有油氣井中進行開採的儲
        new well; and                                                           量；及

(ii)    Through installed extraction equipment and infrastructure       (ii)    當通過除油氣井開採外的其他方式進行開
        operational at the time of the reserves estimate if the                 採，利用儲量估計時點已安裝的開採設備和
        extraction is by means not involving a well.                            基礎設施可開採的儲量。

recovered from new wells on undrilled acreage, or from existing         井中採出的儲量。
wells where a relatively major expenditure is required from
recompletion.

        directly offsetting development spacing areas that are                  域，有一定把握在鑽後能採油，除非證實運

        evidence using reliable technology exists that establishes              上可生產。
        reasonable certainty of economic producibility at greater
        distances.

        reserves only if a development plan has been adopted                    特定環境下須較長時間者除外）鑽井，未鑽

        time.
